 Striking the bridge of the nose is guarantee.

Thirteen

Because of the snowlight, the sky seemed to brighten earlier. As the end of the year approaches, many families buy chickens to feed them, and the crowing sounds are several times louder than usual. Everywhere, roosters crow, and there are scenes of a bountiful year of snow. Xiangzi hadn't slept well all night. In the latter part of the night, he endured several naps, dazed, half-asleep, floating and rising and falling like floating on water, his heart restless. The more he slept, the colder it felt. Hearing the crowing of chickens everywhere, he really couldn't hold on any longer. Not wanting to disturb Old Cheng, he curled up his legs, covered his mouth with the blanket to cough, and didn't dare get up. Enduring and waiting, his heart grew increasingly restless. Finally, when dawn came, the sound of large carts and the shouts of the drivers filled the street, and he sat up. Sitting there was cold too. He stood up, buttoned up, and opened the door crack to look outside. The snow wasn't very thick; it probably stopped falling around midnight; The sky seemed to have cleared, but the gray haze made it hard to see clearly, even the snow had a faint gray shadow. At one glance, he saw the big footprints he had left last night. Although buried again by snow, the holes were still clearly visible.

First, to have some work to do, and second, to erase traces, he didn't say a word, just fumbled for his broom in the corner of the house to sweep the snow. The snow was heavy and hard to sweep, and since he couldn't find a large bamboo broom for a while, he bent his waist low and scraped hard; The upper layer was swept away, leaving some snow on the ground, as if it had grasped the ground. He straightened up twice, swept the entire outer courtyard, and piled the snow beneath two small willow trees. He had a bit of sweat on his body, feeling warm and a bit lighter. He stomped his foot and let out a long, pale breath.

He went inside, put the broom back where it was, and wanted to get up and start packing his bedding. Old Cheng woke up, yawned, and before he could finish speaking, he started talking with his hands; "It's getting late, right?" The tone of his words was very complex. After saying that, he wiped away his tears and casually reached into his leather jacket pocket to take out a cigarette. After taking two drags of his cigarette, he fully understood. "Xiangzi, don't leave yet! Let me go get some hot water, and we'll have a pot of hot tea. This night of bragging is enough for you! ”

"Should I go?" Xiangzi also tried to make peace. But as soon as he said it, he recalled the horror of last night, and suddenly felt a lump in his heart.

"No; Damn! I still have to treat you! Saying that, Old Cheng quickly put on his clothes, buttoning up completely, just tucking a sash over his tattered leather jacket, carrying a cigarette in his mouth as he ran out: "Ha!" The courtyard has been swept all over? You really did it! Please! Xiangzi felt a bit more relieved.

After a while, Old Cheng returned, carrying two big bowls of sweet porridge, along with countless horseshoe baked flatbreads and small tar fried ghosts. "I haven't made any tea, let's have some porridge first. Come on, eat; If it's not enough, buy more; If you don't have money, you can get it on credit; Hard work, just don't pout—come on! ”

The sky was fully bright, and the room was quiet and bright. The two of them held their bowls and drank, the sound loud and sweet. No one said a word, just finished the sesame flatbread and fried dough sticks in one go. "How is it?" Old Cheng picked a sesame seed off his teeth.

"Time to go!" Xiangzi looked at the bedding rolled up on the floor.

"Tell me, I still don't understand what happened!" Old Cheng handed Xiangzi a cigarette, and Xiangzi shook his head.

After thinking it over, Xiangzi felt embarrassed to tell Old Cheng everything. Stammering, he recounted what happened last night. Although it was quite difficult, his account was not entirely incomplete.

Old Cheng pursed his lips for a long time, as if he wanted to catch a little of the flavor. "In my opinion, you should go find Mr. Cao. Things can't just be left untouched, and money can't just be lost! Didn't you just say that Mr. Cao told you to run if things aren't going well? So, if you get out of the car and the detective blocks you, who can you blame? It's not that you're unfaithful, it's that things just came out of hand. You can't help but look out for your own life first! Show me that there's nothing to let down anyone here. Go find Mr. Cao and tell him everything from beginning to end. I believe he definitely can't blame you, and by chance, he might even pay your money!" You should go, leave your bedding here, and go find him early. The days are short, and as soon as the sun rises, it's already eight o'clock. Hurry up and leave! ”

Xiangzi was moved and felt a bit guilty toward Mr. Cao, but Old Cheng made a reasonable point—the detective had blocked him with a gun, so how could he still care about the Cao family's affairs? "Let's go!" Old Cheng urged again. "I think you were a bit evasive last night; When urgent matters arise, no one can keep their head lost. The tricks I'm giving you now are guaranteed to be pretty good. I'm a bit older than you, so I've been through a few more things. Let's go, isn't this the sun just out? ”

A glimmer of morning sunlight, through the snow, illuminates the entire city. Blue sky, white snow—there is light in the sky, light on snow, and between blue and white, a golden flower flashes, making it so exhilarating that you can't keep your eyes open! Just as Xiangzi was about to leave, someone knocked on the door. Old Cheng went out to check and called out from the doorway, "Xiangzi!" Looking for you! ”

Wang Er from the Left Residence, his nose dripping with cold water, stomped off the snow from his feet in the doorway. Old Cheng saw Xiangzi come out and said, "Everyone, sit inside!" The three of them came into the house together.

"Well," Wang Er rubbed his hands and said, "I'm just looking at the house. How do I get in? The front door is locked." Well, after the snow, it's cold, really cold! Well, Mr. Cao and Mrs. Cao both left early in the morning; Going to Tianjin, maybe Shanghai, I can't quite say. Mr. Zuo asked me to come see the house. Well, it's really cold! ”

Xiangzi suddenly felt like crying! Just as I was about to follow Old Cheng's advice and go find Mr. Cao, Mr. Cao would leave. After a long pause, he asked, "Mr. Cao, didn't say anything to me?" ”

"Well, no. Before dawn, everyone was already up, barely able to talk. The train, well, it leaves at 7:40! So, how am I supposed to get to that courtyard? Wang Er was eager to go over.

"Jump over!" Xiangzi glanced at Old Cheng, as if handing Wang Er over to Old Cheng. He picked up his bedding and rolled it up.

"Where are you going?" Old Cheng asked.

"People and factories, there's nowhere else to go!" This sentence fully expresses Xiangzi's grievances, shame, and helplessness. He had no other choice but to surrender! All paths were sealed, so he could only seek out the tower-like Tiger Girl on the snowy white ground. He cares about appearances, is strong-willed, loyal, and loyal; None of it is useless because of a "dog's life"!

Old Cheng took it and said, "You go ahead." This is like this, right in front of Wang Er—you didn't touch a single blade of grass or tree at the Cao residence! Let's go. When you come up to this street, come in and chat for a while. Maybe I'll find out something good and recommend it to you." After you leave, I'll take Wang Er over there. Is there coal? ”

"Coal and firewood—all in the small house in the backyard." Xiangzi picked up and covered the bed.

The snow on the street was no longer so white; the roads were rolled down by wheels, revealing a hint of ice. The dirt paths had been trampled by horses, leaving patches black and white—what a pity. Xiangzi didn't think about anything else and just carried his bedding forward. He went straight to Renhe Garage. He didn't dare to stop; once he did, he knew he wouldn't have the courage to go in. He kept walking inside, his face burning hot. He made up a sentence, intending to say to Huniu: "I'm here, see what you do!" Whatever I do, I have no choice! When he finally saw her, he repeated the words in his mind several times but couldn't say them; his mouth wasn't that convenient.

Huniu had just gotten up, her hair bristling and her eyes swollen, her dark face covered with a layer of white goosebumps, like a plucked-feathered frozen chicken.

"Oh! You're back! "Very affectionate, her eyes sparkled with a smile." Rent me a car! Xiangzi lowered his head to look at some unmelted snow on the tip of his shoe.

"Go talk to the old man." She whispered, then gestured toward the east room.

Master Liu Si was drinking tea inside the room, with a large white stove in front of him, the flames over half a foot high. Seeing Xiangzi come in, he said with a half-annoyed smile, "You brat, where are you still alive?!" Forget me! Count, how many days have you been away? How did things go? Have you bought it on the car yet? ”

Xiangzi shook his head, his heart aching as if it were a pain. "You still need a car to haul me, Fourth Master!"

"Hmph, another blow up!" Alright, go pick one yourself! "Master Liu Si poured a bowl of tea," Come, have a bowl first. ”

Xiangzi picked up his bowl, stood in front of the stove, and drank heartily. The tea was very hot, the fire very hot, and he felt a bit sleepy. He put down the bowl and was about to come out when Liu Siye called him back.

The snow on the ground was swept away, the snow on the roof gradually melted, and the shelter workers called out to the roof to set up the shed frame. Fourteen

The Liu family's affairs were handled with great excitement. Liu Siye was very pleased that so many people came to kowtow and wish him a happy birthday. Even more proud, many old friends also rushed to offer their congratulations. From these old friends, he saw that his project was not only lively but also "improved." Those old friends' attire was outdated, while the Fourth Master's leather robes and mandarin jackets were all newly made. Professionally, several friends were wealthier than him back then, but now—after the changes over the past twenty or thirty years—their lives have fallen lower and lower, and some can barely afford to eat. Looking at them, then at his wedding canopy, the longevity hall, the hanging screens of Changban Slope, and the three bowls of the banquet, he felt he was indeed superior to them—he had 'reformed.' Even when gambling, he prepared mahjong tiles, which was much more refined than betting on bets. Yet, amid this lively atmosphere, he also felt a bit of sorrow and sorrow. Having grown used to living alone, he originally thought the people who came to his birthday would be just the shopkeepers and gentlemen from the shop, and the bachelors he had known outside the house. Unexpectedly, some female guests also showed up. Although Huniu could host him, he suddenly felt lonely—no husband, only a daughter, who looked like a man. If Huniu were a man, of course she would have already married and had children. Even if she was an old widower, she might not be so lonely and helpless. Yes, he lacked nothing except a son. The longer one lives, the slimmer the hope of having a son. Birthday celebrations are supposed to be joyful occasions, yet they seem to require tears. No matter how much he reforms his conscience, if no one continues his career, isn't it all for nothing?

For the first half day, he was very fond of it. When everyone celebrated his birthday, he boasted as if he was a legendary old hero who had won the title of Ao Li. In the second half of the day, his anger subsided a bit. Looking at the children brought by the female guests, he felt both envious and jealous, yet afraid to get close to them, and feeling uncomfortable not getting close to them. He wanted to throw a tantrum, but refused to lose his temper immediately. He knew he was an outsider and couldn't embarrass himself in front of family and friends. He was willing to let this day pass quickly and no longer suffer this suffering.

There was also a minor flaw: during the morning meal for the coachmen, Xiangzi almost got into a fight.

Dinner started after eight, and the coachmen were a bit reluctant. Although they had left the cart allowance for the whole day yesterday, no one came to eat empty-handed today. Whether it was a dime or forty zi, everyone received a share of the money. Usually, everyone is the hardship, Liu Si is the factory owner; Today, from everyone's perspective, they are guests and should not be treated this way.

Besides, after eating, you have to leave. No way to take the cart out—it's the end of the year!

Xiangzi definitely knew he wasn't in the group to leave after eating, but he was willing to eat with everyone. First, they finished eating early and got to work; second, they were noticeably friendly. He sat down with everyone, and everyone shifted all their dissatisfaction toward Liu Si onto him. As soon as we sat down, someone said, "Hey, you're an honored guest—why are you sitting with us?" Xiangzi gave a silly smile, not catching the meaning behind his words. These past few days, he hadn't spoken about gossip himself, so his mind seemed to be less active. No one dared to lose their temper with Liu Si, so they could only take a bite of him; Dishes can't be added, but wine can't be restricted—it's a wedding banquet! They all wanted to use the wine to kill their anger. Some shouted in silence, some guessed the truth and fisted up; Old Liu couldn't stop them from rock-paper-scissors. Xiangzi watched everyone drink, and since he wasn't comfortable going too far, he only drank two cups along with them. As they drank, everyone's eyes turned red and their mouths were no longer controlled. Some said, "Xiangzi, Camel, your job is wonderful!" Enough to eat for a whole day, serving the master and miss! Tomorrow, you don't need to pull the cart, just go with your bag! Xiangzi caught on to some of the meaning but didn't take it to heart; From the moment he joined Renhe Factory, he decided not to play the hero anymore and to leave everything to fate. Whoever wants to say whatever they want, they can say it. He held back his anger. Some added, "Xiangzi is taking a different path. We earn money by strength, but Xiangzi is internal skill!" Everyone burst out laughing. Xiangzi sensed that everyone was "biting" him, but since he had already suffered such a big grievance, why bother with these few gossip words? He hadn't spoken yet. The people at the neighboring table noticed the bargain, some craned their necks and shouted, "Xiangzi, tomorrow you'll be the factory owner, don't forget your buddy!" Before Xiangzi could respond, the person at the table spoke again: "Speak, camel!" ”

Xiangzi blushed and whispered, "How can I be the factory owner?!" "Hmph, why can't you? Just watch it as it starts, thump thump thump crack[1]

 Alright! Xiangzi didn't bother to beat around the bush. What did 'dong dong ka' mean? But his intuition was to guess that it referred to his relationship with Huniu. His face slowly turned pale as he suddenly recalled all the grievances he had suffered in the past, all bottled up inside. Xiangzi? ”

Everyone was stunned. They did intend to "bite" him and leave some idle talk, but they were not prepared to fight.

Suddenly, the silence struck, like a bird crying in the forest suddenly seeing an eagle. Xiangzi stood there alone, much taller than the others, feeling his own isolation. But with anger burning in his heart, he seemed convinced that even if they all fought together, they wouldn't be his match. He added, "Is there anyone who dares to go out?" ”

Suddenly, everyone caught on and almost all at once: "Enough, Xiangzi, I'm just teasing you!" ”

Master Liu saw this and said, "Sit down, Xiangzi!" Then he said to everyone, "Don't bully anyone just because they're honest. If you get anxious, I'll kick you all out!" Eat up! Xiangzi left the table. Everyone teased Old Liu with their eyes and picked up their food. Soon, it started chirping again, like a forest bird whose danger had passed, softly chirping again.

Xiangzi waited at the door for a long time, waiting for them. If any of them dare to gossip again, beat them up! If you lose everything, give it a 'no matter what seedlings' [2]

 Right! But everyone came out in groups of three or five, not looking for him again. Although he didn't win, he still vented a little. Then he thought about it, today's move had offended many people. Usually, I don't have close friends to begin with, so I have nowhere to vent my frustrations; How could I offend anyone again? He felt a bit regretful. The little thing I just ate was lying in my stomach, causing a slight pain. He stood up, thinking, 'Whatever, those people who fight every few days and suffer from famine are also living amusingly.' Does being honest always have its benefits? Thinking this way, he drew a new path for himself. On this path, Xiangzi was completely different from what he had hoped for before. He is someone who makes friends with everyone he meets but takes advantage everywhere, drinks other people's tea, smokes other people's cigarettes, borrows money but doesn't pay it back, doesn't avoid cars, pees on any place, spends all day playing tricks with the patrol officers, and drags him into a "district" to stay for two or three days is nothing. Yes, such a coachman was alive and happy, at least happier than Xiangzi. Alright, be honest, be disciplined, and be strong. Since it's useless, becoming such a scoundrel isn't so bad. Not only is it good, Xiangzi thought, but it also has the spirit of a true hero—fearless of heaven and earth, and absolutely refuses to bow his head and suffer in silence. By the way! This should be done! Bad Gaga is made by good people. Instead, he felt a bit regretful—this fight didn't go through. Fortunately, she was not busy, and from now on, she no longer bows her head to anyone.

Liu Siye's eyes didn't rub any sand. Putting aside everything he had heard and seen before, he had already understood eighty to ninety percent in his heart. He had spent his whole life running around, fought in gangs, knelt on iron chains, and at the last moment, taught a country bumpkin to move everything, along with his daughter and business? There's no such thing as a free deal! Even if there is, there's no way Liu Si could get it! Liu Si has been the type to fart and collapse since childhood!

Around three or four in the afternoon, some birthday visitors arrived. The old man already found it dull; the more the guests praised his strength and talent, the less interesting he felt.

After the lanterns were lit, the guests gradually dispersed, leaving only a dozen or so close friends and close friends still here. Looking at the empty canopy in the courtyard, glowing blue under the Shuiyue Lantern, and the table with the apron removed, the old man felt lonely and bored, as if he had just seen himself dead like this—just a wedding canopy converted into a white canopy. There were no children or grandchildren in mourning or kneeling before the coffin, only unrelated people playing mahjong and keeping vigil at night! He really wanted to drive out the guests who hadn't left yet; Taking advantage of his own vitality, he should show his strength! But in the end, he felt too embarrassed to use his friend's anger. His anger took a detour, and the more he looked at her, the more annoyed he felt. Xiangzi was sitting in the shed, looking all decent, but the scar on his face looked like jade under the light. How the old man looked at this couple, how awkward it was!

Miss Hu was used to being unconventional and quiet, so today she was dressed up from head to toe and had to pretend to entertain guests—not only to win everyone's praise, but also to show off her skills in front of Xiangzi. The old man grew angrier and angrier, even the satisfaction he felt during the day had become his own confusion; Thinking this, he muttered under his breath, accompanied by many curses that were no longer allowed on the streets.

Before the friends had left, Huniu, wanting to save everyone's face, tried to stop her father from acting out. Let him nag about it, let him get past it.

But unexpectedly, the old man kept talking and turned around to her. She decided not to fall for it! He held his birthday celebration, and she had been busy for several days, yet nothing good came of it. She couldn't tolerate it! Sixty-nine or seventy-nine isn't enough, but we still have to be reasonable! She immediately returned it:

"You have to spend money to do things yourself, what does it matter to me?"

Huniu hadn't expected things to fall apart so quickly. Her plan was only halfway done, while the old man had already figured it out! What should be done? Her face flushed, dark and red, with half-faded pink and bright lighting, she looked like a piece of overcooked pig liver—complex and unsightly. She was a bit tired; Stirred by this, he was furious and couldn't come up with any ideas, his mind in turmoil. Alright, let's just go for it—good or bad depends on this hammer! "It's good that we've made things clear today. Let's settle this account. What do you think? I want to hear it! This is your own fault—don't say I'm angry with you! ”

Why don't you even find out! Get lost! Never teach me to see you again, come here looking for a bargain, huh? ”

The old man's voice was too loud, and several coachmen came to watch the commotion. The players thought Liu Siye was quarreling with the coachman again and still refused to look up.

Xiangzi had no convenient mouth and wanted to say much, but not a single word reached his tongue. He stood there dazed, neck straight, swallowing hard. "Get lost!" Get lost! Come here to find a bargain? When I broke it outside, you weren't even there, hmph! The old man was trying to scare Xiangzi just to scare him. In his heart, he hated Xiangzi not as much as he hated his daughter; he was angry and still thought Xiangzi was indeed an honest man.

"Alright, I'm leaving!" Xiangzi had nothing to say and had to leave quickly; No matter what, he couldn't beat them in their bickering.

The coachmen had originally come to watch the spectacle; when they saw Master Liu Si scold Xiangzi, they still remembered the morning scene and felt very satisfied. When they heard the old man sending Xiangzi out, they sided with him again—Xiangzi had suffered so much, burned bridges after crossing rivers, the old man turned his back on him, and they felt sorry for Xiangzi. Some rushed over and asked, "What's wrong, Xiangzi?" "Xiangzi shook his head.

Better to hurry than hurry—she had to catch Xiangzi quickly, and not even the chicken or the egg would be killed! "It's our business—one rope ties two grasshoppers together—no one can escape! Or will you kick both of us out together? Listen to your words? ”

Huniu hadn't expected things to happen so quickly, pulling out her last move so early. Liu Siye was even more unexpected. But at this point, he couldn't back down, especially in front of everyone. "You really have the nerve to say it out. My old face is burning with excitement!" He slapped himself. "Pah! Shameless! ”

The players stopped their hands, feeling something was off, but they were confused and couldn't bring themselves to speak; Some stood upright, while others stared blankly at their cards.

Having said it, Huniu was actually quite pleased: "I'm shameless?" Don't make me talk about you—what crap haven't you done before? This is the first time for me, and it's all your fault: men should marry when they grow up, women should marry when they grow up. You're sixty-nine, living for nothing! "This isn't in front of the public," she pointed around, "Let's figure it out and be clear-headed!" With this wedding canopy, you can handle some more business! ”

"Me?" Liu Siye's face turned pale from red to white, showing all the single-man spirit he had back then: "Even if I set fire to the shed, I can't let you use it!" "Alright!" Huniu's lips trembled, her voice very unpleasant, "When I pack up my bedding and leave, how much will you give me?" ”

"The money is mine. I can give it to whoever I want!" The old man felt a bit sad when he heard his daughter say she was leaving, but to fight this anger, he steeled himself.

"Your money?" I've helped you all these years; Without me, think about it—if you don't spend all your money on wild women, it's no wonder. Let's stick to our conscience! Her eyes found Xiangzi again, "Go ahead!" ”

Xiangzi stood there straight, without a single word to say.

 Dong dong la, the sound of drums and music during a wedding ceremony, symbolizing the wedding.

 Regardless of the seedling, it doesn't matter who it is.

Fifteen

In terms of martial arts, Xiangzi couldn't hit an old man, nor could he hit a girl. His power had nowhere to be used. To act shamelessly is something you can only imagine, but you can't do it. As for Hu Niu, he could easily stomp his foot and run. For this situation, since she had fallen out with her father and was willing to go with him; No one knows what's going on deep inside; on the surface, she sacrifices herself for Xiangzi; In front of everyone, he couldn't help but show a bit of heroic spirit. He had nothing to say and could only stand there, waiting for the truth to come out; At the very least, he had to do this to act like a real man.

The Liu father and daughter were left staring at each other, with nothing left to say; Xiangzi remained silent. The coachmen, no matter who they leaned on, seemed to find it hard to interject. The players had to speak, and the silence was already awkward. However, everyone could only put on a superficial and perfunctory remark, advising both sides not to get too caught up in the fire and to talk slowly—nothing is impossible to overcome. They can only say these things, but they can't solve anything, nor do they want to solve anything. Seeing that neither side would compromise, then, even an honest official finds it hard to settle family matters, so he might as well slip away when the opportunity arises.

Before everyone had finished walking, Miss Hu grabbed Mr. Feng from Tianshun Coal Plant: "Mr. Feng, doesn't your shop have a place?" Let Xiangzi stay for two days first. We handle things quickly and cannot occupy your place forever. Xiangzi, you go with Mr. Feng. See you tomorrow, let's discuss our matters." Let me tell you, when I go out, I still have to ride in the bridal sedan chair and never leave this door! Mr. Feng, I'm handing him over to you. I'll ask you for him tomorrow! ”

Mr. Feng took a deep breath, unwilling to take responsibility. Xiangzi was eager to leave and said, "I can't escape!" ”

Miss Hu glared at the old man, returned to her room, and cried in a sharp voice, locking the door from the inside.

Mr. Feng and the others persuaded Fourth Master Liu to go in as well, while the old man brought out his stamina from outside, urging everyone not to leave, and to have a few more drinks: "Don't worry, everyone, from now on she is her, I am me, and we'll never argue again." Leave her, just pretend I never had such a girl. I've spent my whole life on the sidelines, and she's utterly embarrassed! Twenty years back, I cut both of them alive! Now, let it be; Planning to ask me for a small copper coin, but it's absolutely impossible! Not a single cent! No! Let's see how she survives! Let her taste it, and she'll know whether it's better for her dad or the wild man! Don't go, have another drink! Everyone brushed it off perfunctorily, all eager to avoid the dispute.

Xiangzi went to the Tianshun Coal Factory.

Sure enough, things happened quickly. Huniu rented two small northern rooms in a large courtyard in Maojiawan; I immediately found it, and the wallpaperer smeared everything so white it fell to the ground; I asked Mr. Feng to write a few happy characters and paste them inside the house. Once the house was sealed, she went to fetch a sedan chair: one sedan chair filled with stars, sixteen sounding instruments, no golden lanterns, no attendants. After everything was settled, she herself put on a red silk sedan chair robe; Rushing before the New Year, so you don't have to start needing acupuncture before breaking five. The wedding day was set on the sixth day of the Lunar New Year, which was both a good day and no taboo door. She took care of everything herself, telling Xiangzi that he had to buy new ones from head to toe: "You only get this once in a lifetime!" ”

Xiangzi only had five yuan in his hand!

Huniu glanced again: "What's wrong?" What about the thirty-something yuan I gave you? ”

Xiangzi couldn't hide the truth anymore and told her everything about the Cao residence. She blinked, half believing and half skeptical: "Alright, I don't have time to argue with you. Let's just stick to our own conscience!" Here's fifteen yuan for you! If you don't dress up like a newcomer when the day comes, you have to be careful! ”

On the sixth day, Huniu boarded the bridal sedan chair. No words exchanged with his father, no brothers escorting him, no congratulations from relatives or friends; Only the gongs and drums resounded lively on the streets after the New Year, and the bridal sedan chair steadily passed through Xi'an Gate and Xisi Pailou, stirring envy and feelings among the new-dressed people—especially the shop staff.

Xiangzi wore new clothes bought from Tianqiao, his face flushed, and a small satin hat with a small coin of three coins. He seemed to forget himself, staring blankly at everything, listening to everything, as if he didn't even recognize himself. He moved from a coal shop to a newly framed white house, not knowing what happened: the past was like a coal mill, piled up black; Now, dazed, she entered the bridal chamber, dazzlingly white, with several blood-red wedding characters pasted on it. He felt a kind of mockery, a paleness, a sense of distant frustration, and a suffocating frustration. Inside the room, Huniu's original table, chairs, and bed were set up; The stove and the kitchen table are new; In the corner of the room was a duster made of five-colored feathers. He recognized those tables and chairs, but felt unfamiliar with the stove, the kitchen table, and the feather duster. The old and new objects combined in one place reminded him of the past, yet worried about the future. Everything was at his mercy; he felt both old and new, some ornament, some strange thing; He no longer recognized himself. He couldn't remember to cry, he couldn't remember to smile. His big hands and feet moved around in this small, warm room, like a big rabbit in a little wooden cage, eyes red as he looked outside, looking inside, but all he had was legs to run, unable to escape! Huniu wore a red jacket, her face smeared with white powder and rouge, her eyes darting toward him. He didn't dare look her in the eye. She is also a strange thing that is both old and new—a girl, and also a woman; Like a woman, but also like a man; Like a human, yet like a vicious beast! This beast, dressed in a red jacket, had already caught him and was preparing to deal with him carefully. Anyone could handle him, but this beast was especially formidable—it had to guard him for a moment, glare at him, laugh at him, and hold him tightly, draining all his strength. He couldn't escape. He took off the little satin hat and stared blankly at the red knots on it until his vision blurred—then he turned his face to see the wall covered with red dots, swirling and jumping, with an even bigger, reddish one in the middle and a mischievous grin on her face! On the wedding night, Xiangzi finally understood: Huniu was not pregnant. Like a magician, she explained to him: "If I didn't treat you so badly, why would you nod so wholeheartedly?" I stuffed a pillow into my waistband! Haha, haha! She laughed so hard she cried: "You silly thing!" Don't even mention it, I have done it fair to you; Who are you, and who am I? I was stunned and argued with Dad. "I came with you, aren't you grateful?" The next day, Xiangzi went out very early. Most shops have already opened their doors, but some are still closed. The couplets on the door are still bright red, but the yellow ones hanging coins have been blown apart by the wind. The street was very calm, with plenty of foreign cars, and the coachmen seemed more spirited than usual. They were all wearing new shoes, and there was a piece of red paper stuck to the back of the cart. Xiangzi envied these rickshaw drivers, thinking they looked a bit like celebrating the New Year, while he himself had been stuck in a gourd for several days; They all behaved and mingled together, while he had no livelihood, wandering the streets. He was uneasy about idleness, but when he planned to plan for tomorrow, he had to consult with Huniu—his wife; He was with his wife—such a wife! —Begging for food in hand. Kong had grown so tall, and had so much strength—it was useless. His first priority was to serve his wife—that red jacket with tiger teeth; Things that drain people's essence and blood; He was no longer human, but merely a piece of meat. He lost himself, struggling only in her teeth, like a mouse caught by a cat. He didn't want to discuss it with her; he had to leave; Once he had made up his mind, he decided to give her a chance to leave without saying goodbye. There's nothing to let down about this—she's a monster who can use pillows to perform magic tricks with him! He felt heartbroken. Not only did he want to tear the new clothes apart, but he also wanted to wash himself inside and out in clean water. He felt his whole body was clinged to something unclean, disgusting, making him feel utterly disgusted. He was willing to never see her face again!

Where to go? He has no destination. Usually, when pulling a cart, his legs followed others' mouths; today, his legs were free, and he felt lost inside. Following the West Fourth Archway southward, he exited Xuanwu Gate: the road was so straight, and his heart would never bend. After leaving the city gate and heading south, he saw a bathhouse. He decided to take a shower.

Stripped naked, looking at his own body, he felt deeply ashamed. He went into the pool, and the hot water made his whole body feel numb. He closed his eyes, and a tingling sensation seemed to radiate some accumulated filth. He barely dared to touch himself, his heart empty, sweat beading down his forehead. Only when his breathing grew a bit rapid did he lazily climb up, his whole body flushed red, like a newborn baby. He seemed afraid to just walk out and wrap a big towel around himself, thinking he was ugly; Although sweat beads fell in rapid succession, he still felt unclean—the filth in his heart seemed forever unwashable: in Liu Siye's eyes, in the eyes of everyone who knew him, he was always a woman stealer!

Before the sweat had fully dripped, he hurriedly put on his clothes and ran out. He was afraid everyone would see his nakedness! Leaving the bathhouse, a cool breeze swept through him, and he felt a sense of relief. The streets were much livelier than before. The clear sky brings a touch of light to everyone's face. Xiangzi's heart was still pounding, not knowing where to go. South, east, and even further south, he headed toward the overpass. After the New Year, around nine o'clock, the shopkeeper's apprentices had already finished breakfast and arrived here. All kinds of stalls and venues for performances were set up early and secured. When Xiangzi arrived, circles of people had already gathered, with drums and gongs beating inside. He had no interest in looking at anything; he could no longer smile.

On ordinary days, the people here perform crosstalk, show bears, perform magic tricks, count treasures, sing yangge, perform drum scripts, and practice skills—all of these provide him with real joy, making him laugh wide-eyed. He couldn't bear to leave Beiping; the overpass was half the reason. Whenever he saw the canopy on the pedestrian bridge and the circles of people, he would recall many funny and lovely things. Now he couldn't be bothered to push forward; the laughter on the overpass was no longer his. He avoided the crowd and walked toward a quieter place, feeling reluctant to leave! No, he couldn't leave this lively and lovely place, couldn't leave the overpass, couldn't leave Beiping. Leave? No way out! He still had to go back to her—with her! —Go negotiate. He can't leave, nor can he sit idle; he must take a step back and think, just as everyone must take a step back when they reach a point of desperation. Having endured all kinds of grievances, why take this point so seriously? He couldn't fix his past, so he could only follow the path downward.

He stopped and listened to the chaotic voices and the sound of gongs and drums; Watching the people and carriages coming and going, I suddenly thought of those two small cottages. The voice in his ears seemed to vanish, the figure before him seemed to vanish, leaving only those two white, warm, small rooms with red wedding characters standing squarely before him. Although they only stayed one night, they were very familiar and intimate. Even that woman in the red jacket seemed not someone to be easily abandoned. Standing on the overpass, he has nothing, is nothing; In those two small rooms, he had everything. Going back—only by going back can there be a way. Everything for tomorrow is in that little room. Shame, fear of trouble, sadness—none of it works; If you plan to survive, you have to find a place where you can find a way.

He hurried back and entered the room, probably just after eleven o'clock. Huniu had already prepared lunch: steamed steamed buns, simmered cabbage with meatballs, a plate of tiger skin jelly, and a plate of braised radish. Everything else was already arranged, except for the cabbage, still simmering over the fire, giving off a wonderful aroma. She had already taken off her red jacket and put on her usual cotton pants and jacket. On her head was a small red flower made of velvet, and on the flower was a small gold paper ingot. Xiangzi glanced at her; she didn't look like a newlywed woman. Every move she made was like that of a longtime daughter-in-law—efficient, mature, and with a hint of self-satisfaction. Although she didn't look like a new bride, she still made him sense something new; She cooks and tidies the house; The scent and the heating in the room were things he had never experienced before. No matter what she did, he felt he had a home. Every home has its own charm. He didn't know what to do.

"Where are you going? You! She asked while serving cabbage. Went to take a shower. He took off his robe.

"Ah! From now on, when you go out, say a word! Don't just wave your hand and walk away so carelessly! He said nothing.

"Can snort but not not?" No, I'll teach you! ”

He snorted, no choice! He knew he had married a female demon, but this demon could cook, tidy up the house, scold him, and help him. No matter what he did, it was not the same as it was! He started eating the steamed bun. The food was indeed tastier than usual, hot and hot; But the food wasn't tasty, and as he chewed, he couldn't feel the usual pleasure of wolfing down food—he couldn't sweat from eating.

After finishing his meal, he lay down on the kang, resting his head in his palm, staring at the roof. "Hey! Help farm the gear! I'm not anyone's boss girl! She called out from the outer room.

Lazy as he was, he stood up, glanced at her, and walked over to help. He was usually very diligent, but now he was holding back his anger as he got things done. Back at the car factory, he often helped her, but now the more he looked at her, the more he hated her. He never hated people as much as he hated her, and he couldn't say why. Angry but unwilling to explode, holding everything in their heart; He couldn't cut ties with her completely; arguing was pointless. Pacing around the small room, he felt his entire life was a story of grievance.

After finishing her things, she glanced around and sighed. He smiled right after. "How is it?"

"What?" Xiangzi squatted by the stove, warming his hands; My hands weren't cold, but since there was nowhere to put them, I had to roast them. These two small rooms really felt like home, but he didn't know where to put his hands and feet.

"Take me out for a play?" Ascend to Baiyun Temple? No, it's late; Strolling down the street? "She wants to fully enjoy the happiness of her newlyweds." Although the marriage didn't look like a good thing, being so carefree was actually nice—it was a good chance to spend more time with her husband and enjoy a few days of fun and fun. At her parents' home, she never lacks food, clothes, or change; But there was no man who truly understood her. Now, she wanted to make up for what she owed, swaggering around the streets, at temple fairs, and going to play with Xiangzi.

Xiangzi refused to leave. First, he thought it was shameful to wander the world with his wife; second, he thought that a wife who came this way could only be hidden at home; This is not something to be proud of; the less you show it off in front of everyone, the better. Also, once you go out, you inevitably run into acquaintances. Everyone in the western half of the city knows Huniu and Xiangzi, so he can't invite everyone to gossip behind his back.

"Can we discuss it?" He was still squatting there.

"What is there to discuss?" She leaned over and stood by the stove. He took his hand away, placed it on his lap, and stared blankly at the flame. After a long stunned silence, he finally said, "I can't just sit around like this!" ”

"A life of suffering!" She chuckled. "If you don't pull a rickshaw for a day, you start itching all over, right? Look at the old man—he's been playing around his whole life, and even in his old age, he still runs a car factory. He neither pulled a rickshaw nor worked hard; he made a living by his heart. You should learn from this. Who does it make you think you are pulling a rickshaw all your life? Let's play for a few days first. Things aren't just busy these days, so why rush around? I don't plan to argue with you these past couple of days, so don't let it take your time to get mad at me! ”

"Let's discuss it first!" Xiangzi decided not to give in. If you can't just stomp your foot and leave, you have to find a way to get things done. You must first stand at one end and not swing back and forth like a swing.

"Alright, go ahead!" She moved a stool over and sat by the stove. "How much money do you have?" He asked.

"Isn't that so? I knew you were going to ask this! You're not marrying a wife, you're paying for a little money, right? ”

Xiangzi felt as if he had been choked by a breath of wind, swallowing several deep breaths. Old Liu and the factory coachman all thought he was greedy and had gotten involved with Huniu; Now, she said it herself! His own car, his own money, lost for no reason, now buried under his wife's few yuan; Even eating has to go down smoothly! He wished he could grab her neck with both hands, strangle! Pinch it! Pinch it! Until she rolled her eyes! He strangled everything, then slit his own throat. They are not human, they must die; He himself is not human, yet he dies; No one needs to think about survival!

Xiangzi stood up, wanting to go out for a walk again; I shouldn't have come back just now. Seeing Xiangzi's expression change, she softened a bit: "Alright, I'll tell you." I have about five hundred yuan in total. Lian Sedan Chair, Rented House—Three Parts [1]

 Burning the shed, making clothes, buying things, bringing them to you, after packing up the bags, I spent nearly a hundred yuan, leaving about four hundred yuan. I'm telling you, you don't need to rush. Let's give it a joyful moment. What about you? Even as an adult, pulling a cart and sweating foully, you should still look good and enjoy a few days; As for me, having been an old lady for so many years, I should enjoy a few more days. When we're almost all spent, we'll still beg the old man. As for me, if I hadn't fallen out with him that day, I definitely wouldn't have gotten over it. Now I'm gone—dad is still dad. As for him, he only has me as his daughter, and you're the one he loves. We should back down a bit and apologize to him. Maybe there's nothing we can't get over it." How ready-made this is! He has money, so we should bear it properly and properly, there's nothing unreasonable about it; You'd be better off being someone else's livestock! In a couple of days, you go first; He might not see you. Once you don't see me, you go a second time; He gave him all the face, so he couldn't help but change his mind. Then I'll go again, at least say a nice word to him, maybe we'll all move back. Once we move back, the security guard holds his head high, and no one dares to look at us sideways; If we keep enduring it here, we'll always be a pair of black people, don't you think? ”

Xiangzi never thought of this. Ever since Huniu came to the Cao residence to find him, he thought he had married her, used her money to buy a car, and then go fetch it himself. Although using his wife's money isn't very respectable, their relationship is one where words can't be spoken, so there's nothing he can do. He hadn't expected Huniu to have such a move. Pulling the long face down, of course it was an idea, but Xiangzi wasn't that kind of person. After thinking it over, he seemed to understand a bit: if he had money, he could teach others to take it away for free, leaving no one to complain to. When someone else gives you money, you have to accept it; Once accepted, you can no longer treat yourself as a human being. You have only a broad mind and strength, but you must be someone else's slave: a plaything for your wife, a servant to your father-in-law. A person seems like nothing at all, just a bird; if you try to catch food yourself, you'll end up in the net. If you eat their grain, you have to obediently stay in the cage, singing for them, and can be sold off at any moment!

He refused to go find Liu Siye. With Huniu, it's like flesh within flesh; It had nothing to do with Liu Si. Having already suffered from her, she couldn't go sue her father again! "I don't want to sit idle!" He only said this one sentence, to avoid unnecessary conversation and arguments.

"A life of hardship, huh!" She said, tapping and teasing him. "Don't like idleness, go do some business."

"I won't!" No money to make! I can pull carts, I love pulling carts! The tendons on Xiangzi's head twitched.

"Let me tell you, you're not allowed to pull a rickshaw!" I won't allow you to sweat all over and get smelly on my kang! You have your ideas, I have mine. Look, let's see who can be more awkward than whom! You married a wife, but I spent it, and you didn't give me a single penny. Think about it—who is we, and should listen to whom? ”

Xiangzi was speechless again.

Sixteen

When the Lantern Festival arrived, Xiangzi couldn't hold back any longer.

Huniu was very happy. She cooked yuanxiao, made dumplings, visited temples during the day, and lanterns at night. She forbade Xiangzi from making any suggestions, but the old man never lacked his appetite and planned to buy him some fresh food to eat. The large courtyard had seven or eight households, most living in a single room; Some rooms were occupied by seven or eight households, young and old. Some of these people pulled carts, some ran small businesses, some became patrol officers, and some served as servants. Everyone had their own matters; no one had a free time. Even the children carried small baskets, making porridge in the morning and collecting coal cores in the afternoon. Only the tiny child would play or fight in the yard with a red-red bottom. Dust from the stove, dirt, and water all poured into the courtyard, with no one bothering to clean up. The middle of the yard was covered in ice, and the older kids picked up coal pits to use as ice rinks, shouting about cracking ice and playing around. The ones suffering the most are the elderly and women. The elderly have neither food nor clothing, lying on the cold kang, waiting for the young to earn some money and enjoy a bowl of porridge. The hardworking young might earn money, or maybe return empty-handed, only to lose their temper and argue with every opportunity. The elderly, on an empty stomach, had to treat their tears as water and swallow them down. Those women had to look after both the elderly and the young, and also had to brush off the young, earning money. Even while pregnant, they still had to do things as usual, eating only cornbread and sweet potato porridge; No, not only did they have to work as usual, but they also had to make porridge and take on some chores—fortunately, the young and old were all full and lay down, so they had to carry a small kerosene lamp to wash, work, and mend for others. The room was so small, the walls so broken, and cold wind seeped in through the cracks on one side of the wall and kept going out from the other, taking away all the warmth. They only wore some tattered cloth hanging from their bodies, had a bowl or half a bowl of porridge in their bellies, or had a fetus six or seven months old. They had to work, and first had to make sure everyone had enough to eat. They were covered in illness, losing their hair before thirty, but couldn't be idle for a moment, walking from illness to death; When they die, the coffin must be donated to the "good people." Those girls, sixteen or seventeen years old, without pants, could only wrap around some broken thing in the house—a natural prison—helping their mother with chores and rushing to work. To get to the outhouse, they had to make sure the courtyard was empty before daring to run out like thieves; All winter, they never saw the sun or the blue sky. The ugly one will inherit everything from their mother in the future; Those who look decent even know that sooner or later their parents will sell them off and "enjoy life"!

It was in such a courtyard that Huniu felt very proud. She was the only one who had food and clothes, no need to rush, and could walk around and explore. She lifted her face high, coming and going, feeling both her superiority and afraid others might mess with her, so she ignored those suffering people. Those who come here to do small business are almost all selling cheap things—scraped meat, frozen cabbage, raw fermented bean juice, donkey and horse meat—all come here to find their caretakers. Ever since Huniu moved in, vendors selling lamb head meat, smoked fish, hard dough buns, and braised fried tofu have all called out at the door. She held her bowl, her face up, bringing the snacks into the room. The children all stuck their iron-like fingers in their mouths, looking at her as if she were some kind of princess. She came to enjoy life; she couldn't, refused, and didn't want to see others' hardships.

Xiangzi first looked down on her actions; he was a poor boy who knew what hardship meant. He didn't want to eat those trivial things, but unfortunately, that money was wasted. Second, and even more embarrassing for him, he figured out something: she forbade him from pulling the cart, but kept him fed with good food every day, just like fattening up a cow to milk cows! He had completely become her thing. He had seen a thin, old on the street, when running errands, also choose a fat male dog. Thinking of this, he not only hated this way of life, but also worried about himself. He knew how a hardworking man should protect his body—the body is everything. If he lived like this, one day he would become a dry skeleton, still so big, with an empty chest. He began to tremble. Planning to be deadly, he had to immediately pull a rickshaw, go out for a run, run all day, come back and fall asleep immediately, unaware of the situation; If he didn't eat her good things, he wouldn't serve her to play. He decided to do it this way and could not make any further concessions; She's willing to pay for a car, okay; She didn't want to; he would hire a car to pick her up. Without a word, he decided to go hire a car. On the seventeenth, he started pulling a rickshaw, renting it "all day." After running two longer trades, he noticed some ailments he hadn't had before—tightness in his calves, aching in his hips. He knew where his illness was, but to comfort himself, he thought it was probably because he hadn't pulled a rickshaw for over twenty days, and his leg had grown out; After a few trips, just spread your legs and maybe everything will be fine.

He pulled up another deal, this time with a helping cart, four of them traveling together. They grabbed the bike handle, and everyone let a tall man in his forties lead the way. The tall man smiled and realized that those three cars were all "better" than himself. He had put in all his strength. Although he knew he couldn't outrun the three young men behind him, he refused to rely on his seniority. After running more than a mile, he praised him, saying, "What's wrong? Do you need energy?" They really wouldn't part! He replied, panting, "Driving with your guys, is it okay if you're slow?!" "He really ran fast; even Xiangzi had to push seventy to eighty percent of his strength just to keep up." His running style was not impressive: tall and unable to slump his waist, his waist and back seemed like a flat plank, so his whole body had to lunge forward; When the body moves forward, the hands are noticeably placed backward; It wasn't like running, but more like dragging something forward to crawl forward. If the waist is stiff, his hip bones must move; His feet were almost pulling on the ground, twisting forward even faster. He twisted quite fast, but just by looking, it was obvious he was struggling with great effort. At the twists and turns, he would brace himself and twist hard, and everyone was sweating for him; He always seemed to just keep moving forward, regardless of whether the car could pass or not.

When he did, sweat was dripping steadily from his nose tip, ears, and lips. After getting out of the car, he quickly straightened up and grinned. When he took the money, his hands were shaking so much he could barely hold onto something.

Walking together for a ride counts as friends, and the four of them parked their cars together. Xiangzi wiped his sweat and continued joking as usual. The tall man paced alone for a long time, coughing for a long time and spitting out a lot of white foam before finally regaining some relief and starting to talk to them: "It's over!" And that heart; My waist and legs are all out of strength! No matter how much you lift your waist, you can't lift your legs; Desperate! ”

"He didn't leave those two steps just now. You think he was slow!" A short, short-statured man in his twenties took it and said, "Unyielding, all three of us are great—who hasn't sweated?" The tall man looked a bit proud, but also ashamed, and sighed.

"Take your running style, you know, it's almost like you'd get taught to puncture yourself[1]

 Do you believe it? "Another young man said." Getting old is not just talking. The tall man smiled and shook his head: "Isn't it all about age, buddy!" Let me tell you the truth: in our line of work, don't start a family, really! Seeing everyone leaning in their ears, he lowered his voice: "Yicheng family, day and night is never idle, it's over!" Look at my waist, it's done, not a shred of vitality! Better not rush around—if you grit your teeth and start coughing, your chest feels like a burning sensation! Forget it, anyone in our line of work has to be single for life! Even the damn little birds are paired one by one, and we're not allowed to start a family! Another story is that after starting a family, I have one child a year, but now I have five! Everyone is waiting to eat with their mouths open! The train is big, grain is expensive, and the business is tough—what can be done! Better to be single for life, then go to the White House and get a big bayberry sor, and accept your fate! One person, if he dies, he dies! Once this thing becomes a family, both big and small, several bites, you can't close your eyes even in death! Don't you agree? He asked Xiangzi.

Xiangzi nodded but said nothing.

At this moment, a seat arrived. The short guy agreed first, but he gave it up and called out to the tall guy, "Big brother, you can take the seat!" There are five kids at home! ”

The tall man laughed, "Alright, I'll go again!" Normally, that's not the case! Alright, I'll bring back a few more pancakes later! See you later, buddy! Watching the tall man walk away, the short man muttered to himself, "Damn it, I've been hanging around for a lifetime and haven't even found a wife!" Inside his damn house, each person is holding four or five women! ”

"Let's not mention him for now," another young man chimed in. "Look at those who make a living, you really have to be careful. The tall guy wasn't wrong. Just put it this way: why start a family? Can you just leave it as a toy to watch? No! Alright, this is Louzi! Eating cornbread all day, attacking from both sides—even the best young man has to climb down! Hearing this, Xiangzi pulled the cart up and chatted, saying, "Put it south, there's no business here." ”

"See you!" The two young ones said together.

Xiangzi seemed not to hear. Walking and kicking as he went, his hip shaft was indeed a bit sore! I wanted to stop pulling the truck, but I simply didn't have the courage to go home. The one at home isn't a wife, but a blood-sucking demon!

The sky was slowly growing longer. He wandered around two or three times, and it was only just past five o'clock. He handed over the car and spent a while longer in the teahouse. After drinking two pots of tea, he felt hungry and decided to eat his fill outside before heading home. After eating twelve taels of meat pies and a bowl of red bean millet porridge, I burped as I slowly walked home. He knew there was a thunderbolt waiting for him at home, but he remained calm; He made up his mind: he wouldn't argue with her, wouldn't make a fuss, would fall asleep as soon as he was headed, and come out tomorrow as usual to pull the rickshaw—she could do whatever she wanted!

As soon as he entered, Huniu was sitting in the outer room. She glanced at him, her face so dark it looked like water was about to drip. Xiangzi planned to smooth things over, pulled his long face aside, and called out to her. But he wasn't used to such things, so he lowered his head and walked into the inner room. She didn't make a sound; the small room was as quiet as an ancient mountain cave. The coughs, talks, and cries of the neighbors in the courtyard were all heard very clearly, as if they were far away, as if hearing distant sounds from a mountain.

Neither of them would speak first, keeping their mouths shut and lying down one after another, like two silent turtles. After waking up, Huniu spoke, her voice tone half annoyed and half amused: "Where have you been?" It took a whole day to leave! ”

"Off to pull the cart!" He said, half-asleep and half-awake, as if something was stuck in his throat. "Oh! If you don't sweat a sweat, your heart itches—you wretched bone! The dishes I cooked for you, and you don't come back to eat them. Are you going to travel around the world for comfort? Don't mess me up. My dad came from a bachelor background—I'm capable of anything! If you dare go out again tomorrow, I'll hang myself to show you. If I can say it, then I can do it!" "I can't just sit idle!"

"Why don't you go find the old man?"

"No!"

"So arrogant!"

Xiangzi was truly fired up. He couldn't hold back his thoughts and couldn't hold back any longer: "Pull the cart, buy your own rickshaw. If anyone stops me, I'll leave, never to come back!" "Mm—" The sound was long and winding in her nose. In this voice, she expressed pride and disdain for Xiangzi, but inside she was also taking a twist. She knew Xiangzi was—though honest—a tough guy. The tough guy's words are always meant to be playful. It was hard enough to catch him; he couldn't just let go so easily. He is an ideal person: honest, thrifty, and robust; Given her looks and age, it's really hard to get another treasure like her. Being both strong and gentle is true skill. She had to give him a hard time: "I know you're strong-willed, but you have to know I truly care about you." If you refuse to go find the old man, here's what you do: I'll go find him. After all, she was his daughter, so losing face was no big deal. ”

"If the old man wants us, I still have to pull the rickshaw!" Xiangzi got straight to the point.

Huniu was silent for a long time. She hadn't expected Xiangzi to be so clever. Although his words were simple, it was clear he was no longer falling for her trap; he was not a fool. That's why she found it all the more interesting. She had to put in some effort to hold on to this adult who would falter when anxious, or something big. She couldn't push too hard; finding such a big thing wasn't easy. She had to loosen and tighten a little, so he could never escape her grasp. "Alright, you love pulling carts, but I can't either. You have to swear not to hire a chartered car, and you have to come back every day; Look, if I go a day without seeing you, I'll start to panic! Promise me, you'll come home early every night! ”

Xiangzi remembered what the tall man said earlier that day! Opening my eyes to the darkness, I saw a group of cart pullers, small traders, laborers, their backs unable to slump and their legs tugging at their feet. He will be like that in the future. But he couldn't afford to be awkward with her any longer; as long as he could pull the cart there, he would already have a victory. "I keep pulling out of the seat!" He agreed.

Although she said that, she wasn't very enthusiastic about going to see Master Liu Si. Father and daughter naturally bickered often, but now things were different. She couldn't just say a few words and let the clouds disappear overnight, because she was no longer considered part of the Liu family. Women who marry tend to be somewhat distant from their parents at home. She didn't dare to walk straight into the courtroom and leave. If the old man really turns his back on her, she'll make a scene. If he refuses to let go of the property, she'll have nothing to do. Even when someone was mediating nearby, when she was helpless, they could only persuade her to come back—she had a home of her own.

Xiangzi went to pull the rickshaw as usual, pacing alone inside the house, repeatedly wanting to get dressed and go find her father, but her mind was too lazy to move. She was in trouble. For your own comfort and happiness, you must go back; For one's own dignity, not going is the right choice. If the old man calmed down, she could just bring Xiangzi to the Renhe factory, teaching him to have work so he wouldn't have to pull rickshaws anymore, and he could steadily take over his father's business. Her heart lit up. What if the old man was determined to the end? She lost face—no, not only was she embarrassed, but she also had to accept being a coachman's wife; She, hmph! There was no difference from the group of women in the courtyard. Suddenly, darkness filled her heart. She almost regretted marrying Xiangzi. No matter how strong-willed he was, if her father didn't agree, he would have been a rickshaw puller his whole life. Thinking of this, she even wanted to return to her parents' home alone and sever ties with Xiangzi, refusing to lose everything for him. Then he thought, the joy of following Xiangzi is beyond words. She sits on the kang, dazed and distant, longing for the happiness after marriage; Its whole body looks like a big red flower, its fragrance warmly blooming under the sunlight. No, I can't bear to part with Xiangzi. No matter if he pulled a rickshaw or begged, he always had to follow him. Look, look at the women in the courtyard—if they can endure it, then so can she. If it disperses, she won't want to leave the Liu family.

Since leaving Renhe Factory, Xiangzi refused to walk Xi'anmen Street again. These past couple of days, whenever he pulled a rickshaw, he always headed straight to the East City, so the West City wouldn't be full of people and factory trucks, and he'd feel awkward running into them. That day, after collecting the car, he deliberately passed by the factory gate, not for anything else, just to catch a glimpse. Huniu's words still lingered in his mind, as if he wanted to test whether he had the courage to return to the factory. If Huniu could say something good to the old man; Before returning to the factory, I first tried walking down this street if I dared not to do so. He pulled his hat down a bit and slipped away toward the factory, afraid that someone he knew would see him. From afar, he saw the car door light and somehow felt very sad inside. He recalled how he first arrived here, remembered Huniu's temptation, and remembered the night of his birthday. All of this is very clear, like pictures floating before your eyes. Between these paintings, there are also some clear and brief ones nestled between these paintings: Western Hills, Camel, Cao Residence, Detective...... Everything is clear, terrifying, and connected into a single unity. The pictures were so clear that he felt somewhat lost, almost as if he were really looking at a few paintings and forgot he was inside too. But when he thought about his relationship with them, his mind became restless. Suddenly, they spun up, down, left, and right, scattered and confused. He couldn't recall why he deserved such torment and grievance. These scenes seemed to take up a long time, yet also a short time; he couldn't figure out how old he really was. He felt that he had aged a lot compared to when he first joined Renhe Factory. At that time, his heart was full of hope; Now, my stomach is filled with worry. He didn't understand why, but these pictures would never deceive him.

Right in front of him was Renhe Factory. He stood on the other side of the street, staring blankly at the dazzlingly bright electric light. As he read, a sudden thought struck him. The four golden characters under the lamp—People and Car Factory—have changed! He couldn't read, but he remembered what the first character looked like: like two sticks joined together, neither a fork nor a triangle—such a simple and strange character. If you find the word by sound, it's probably "person." This "person" has changed its appearance to "ren"—a word even stranger than "person." He couldn't think of any reason. Looking again at the east and west rooms—the two rooms he could never forget—there were no lights on.

He stood so impatiently that he lowered his head and walked home. As I walked, I wondered if the people and the factory had collapsed. He had to slowly inquire and didn't want to say anything to his wife for now. Back home, Huniu was inside cracking sunflower seeds to pass the time. "So late again!" There was not a trace of goodwill on her face. "Let me tell you, if this keeps up, I can't take it! You go out all day, and I don't even dare to move my nest. The whole yard is full of poor people, afraid of losing something. All day long, there's nowhere to say a word. No, I'm not a wooden figure. Come up with your own ideas, this won't do! Xiangzi didn't utter a word.

"Say something! What's with deliberately teasing someone's fire? Do you have a mouth? Do you have a mouth? Her words grew faster and crisper, firing like a small cannon in succession. Xiangzi still had nothing to say.

"That's enough," she said, truly anxious, but also a bit helpless about him. Her face was neither crying nor smiling, so anxious she couldn't help but lose control. "Let's buy two cars and rent them out. Can you stay home and eat at the car portion?" Is it okay? "Two cars earn thirty cents a day, not enough food!" Rent one, I'll pull one myself, just make do! Xiangzi spoke slowly, but it was very natural; When he heard about buying a car, he forgot everything.

"Isn't that the same? You're still not home! ”

"That works too," Xiangzi's idea seemed to follow the car issue, "just rent one out and get it for the whole day." That one, I pulled it myself for half a day, then rented it out for another half day. If I had to go out during the day, I'd go out early and be back by three o'clock; "I want to take Wan'er along. I won't leave until three o'clock and will be back at night." Pretty good! "She nodded." Let me think about it. If I don't have a better idea, I'll just do it. ”

Xiangzi was very happy. If this idea could be realized, he would have pulled his own car again. Although it was bought by his wife, as he gradually saved money, he could still buy another car himself. It was only then that he realized Huniu had some merits. He actually smiled at her—an innocent, heartfelt smile that seemed to wipe away all past hardships, and that smiling brought him to a new world—as easy as changing clothes—refreshing!

 Pout, losing. Defeated.
